A non-empty lane requires current_plan_slug. Pass [] with a plan to release your whole lane; pass [] without a plan for a repl…; declared_goal_refs?:array(1-40)<string> Optional typed goal refs explicitly covered by this declaration — generated goal slugs and WI-/EI- work-item refs are accepted; blank and bare numeric refs are rejected. Omit when no multi-goal declaration is intended; prose is never infer…; harness?:string(≤120) Harness the plan lives in, for the item claims (default: session harness, or operator home if unscoped).; current_files?:array<string> Repo-relative files currently in scope — read by lock-contention enrichment (is the holder still focused on this path?) and by fleet placement. Omit to leave your declared file set untouched; pass [] to clear it. Same omission semantics as…; ambient_excluded_refs?:array<string> Work-item/session refs that ambient corpus retrieval must never inject into this session. Omit to preserve the durable fence; pass [] to clear it. A read failure fails closed for corpus retrieval.\",\"returns\":null,\"returnsSource\":null,\"via\":\"both\"}],\"howToCall\":\"activated:false — these tools are NOT on your live tool surface, so a direct call will fail with unknown/not-found. Orthogonal to full:true, which controls body excerpts.\\n  all?: boolean;\\n  // Return COMPLETE fact bodies instead of excerpts. Use when you need a fact verbatim, ideally with a tighter `limit`.\\n  full?: boolean;\\n  // Alias for scopeRef when scope:\\\"harness\\\" (EI-7371) — prefer scopeRef.\\n  harness?: string;\\n  // With versions:true, the single fact key whose history to read; without versions, query ONE live fact directly (useful when a bounded list cannot establish absence).\\n  key?: string;\\n  // Max facts (default 4; positive values are clamped to 4 for ordinary result-door pages).
